 // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                         \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, __LINE__);             \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                                    val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread: gather flux from connected elements and\n// integrate. The device only holds this rank's slice: elements_static covers\n// the owned range [elem_begin, elem_end), elements_in/out cover it plus one\n// halo row on each side starting at global index alloc_begin. Connectivity\n// indices are global and are rebased onto the slice.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elements_static,\n                             const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_in,\n                             ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_out,\n                             const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n                             const size_t elem_begin, const size_t elem_end,\n                             const size_t alloc_begin) {\n    const size_t i = elem_begin + blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= elem_end) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& elem_static = elements_static[i - elem_begin];\n    const ElementDynamic elem_dyn = elements_in[i - alloc_begin];\n    const Material mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements\n    for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n        const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n        const ElementDynamic neighbor_dyn = elements_in[neighbor_idx - alloc_begin];\n        total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    ElementDynamic& elem_write = elements_out[i - alloc_begin];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Row-block domain decomposition: rank owns grid rows [row_begin, row_end).\nstruct Decomposition {\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    int row_begin = 0;\n    int row_end = 0;\n    size_t elem_begin = 0;\n    size_t elem_end = 0;\n};\n\nDecomposition decompose(const int n_elems_root) {\n    Decomposition d;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.size);\n\n    const int rows_per_rank = n_elems_root / d.size;\n    const int remainder = n_elems_root % d.size;\n    d.row_begin = d.rank * rows_per_rank + std::min(d.rank, remainder);\n    d.row_end = d.row_begin + rows_per_rank + (d.rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n    d.elem_begin = (size_t)d.row_begin * n_elems_root;\n    d.elem_end = (size_t)d.row_end * n_elems_root;\n    return d;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations. Each rank advances its row block on\n// its GPU; boundary rows are exchanged with neighboring ranks each iteration.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters, const Decomposition& d,\n                   const int n_elems_root) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const size_t row_bytes = (size_t)n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_owned = d.elem_end - d.elem_begin;\n\n    // Device buffers hold the full global index space so connectivity indices\n    // can be used directly; only the owned block plus halo rows stay current.\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    Material* d_materials = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dyn = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dyn_swap = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_static, n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dyn, n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dyn_swap, n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_static, world.elements_static.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_materials, world.materials.data(),\n                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn, world.elements_dynamic.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Pinned staging buffers for halo rows: [0] = row sent down/received from\n    // below (row_begin - 1), [1] = row sent up/received from above (row_end).\n    ElementDynamic* h_send = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* h_recv = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_send, 2 * row_bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recv, 2 * row_bytes));\n\n    // Ranks with no rows (size > n_elems_root) sit out the halo exchange;\n    // empty blocks only occur at the tail of the rank range, so a non-empty\n    // rank's index neighbors are always its rank neighbors.\n    const bool has_work = n_owned > 0;\n    const int prev_rank = (has_work && d.row_begin > 0) ? d.rank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    const int next_rank = (has_work && d.row_end < n_elems_root) ? d.rank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n\n    constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n    const int grid_size = has_work ? (int)((n_owned + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE) : 0;\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Refresh halo rows with neighbors' results from the previous\n        // iteration (initial state is globally consistent on every rank).\n        if (iter > 0 && d.size > 1) {\n            if (has_work) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send, d_dyn + d.elem_begin, row_bytes,\n                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send + n_elems_root,\n                                      d_dyn + d.elem_end - n_elems_root, row_bytes,\n                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            }\n            MPI_Request reqs[4];\n            MPI_Irecv(h_recv, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, prev_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[0]);\n            MPI_Irecv(h_recv + n_elems_root, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, next_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[1]);\n            MPI_Isend(h_send, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, prev_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[2]);\n            MPI_Isend(h_send + n_elems_root, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, next_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[3]);\n            MPI_Waitall(4, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n            if (prev_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn + d.elem_begin - n_elems_root, h_recv,\n                                      row_bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n            if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn + d.elem_end, h_recv + n_elems_root,\n                                      row_bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Update all owned elements\n        if (has_work) {\n            updateKernel<<<grid_size, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_static, d_dyn, d_dyn_swap,\n                                                    d_materials, d.elem_begin, d.elem_end);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n        }\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_dyn, d_dyn_swap);\n    }\n\n    // Gather all owned blocks onto every rank so the final state matches the\n    // serial code's elements_dynamic everywhere.\n    if (has_work) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data() + d.elem_begin,\n                              d_dyn + d.elem_begin, n_owned * sizeof(ElementDynamic),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n    if (d.size > 1) {\n        std::vector<int> counts(d.size), displs(d.size);\n        const int rows_per_rank = n_elems_root / d.size;\n        const int remainder = n_elems_root % d.size;\n        for (int r = 0; r < d.size; ++r) {\n            const int rb = r * rows_per_rank + std::min(r, remainder);\n            const int re = rb + rows_per_rank + (r < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n            counts[r] = (int)((size_t)(re - rb) * n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n            displs[r] = (int)((size_t)rb * n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n        }\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_BYTE, world.elements_dynamic.data(),\n                       counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_BYTE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_send));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_recv));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_static));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_materials));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dyn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dyn_swap));\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n\n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification.\n// XOR-combining is order-independent, so the loop parallelizes bit-exactly.\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    const size_t n = elements.size();\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            int rank0;\n            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank0);\n            if (rank0 == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                                    val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread: gather flux from connected elements and\n// integrate. The device only holds this rank's slice: elements_static covers\n// the owned range [elem_begin, elem_end), elements_in/out cover it plus one\n// halo row on each side starting at global index alloc_begin. Connectivity\n// indices are global and are rebased onto the slice.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elements_static,\n                             const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_in,\n                             ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_out,\n                             const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n                             const size_t elem_begin, const size_t elem_end,\n                             const size_t alloc_begin) {\n    const size_t i = elem_begin + blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= elem_end) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& elem_static = elements_static[i - elem_begin];\n    const ElementDynamic elem_dyn = elements_in[i - alloc_begin];\n    const Material mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements\n    for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n        const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n        const ElementDynamic neighbor_dyn = elements_in[neighbor_idx - alloc_begin];\n        total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    ElementDynamic& elem_write = elements_out[i - alloc_begin];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Row-block domain decomposition: rank owns grid rows [row_begin, row_end).\nstruct Decomposition {\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    int row_begin = 0;\n    int row_end = 0;\n    size_t elem_begin = 0;\n    size_t elem_end = 0;\n};\n\nDecomposition decompose(const int n_elems_root) {\n    Decomposition d;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.size);\n\n    const int rows_per_rank = n_elems_root / d.size;\n    const int remainder = n_elems_root % d.size;\n    d.row_begin = d.rank * rows_per_rank + std::min(d.rank, remainder);\n    d.row_end = d.row_begin + rows_per_rank + (d.rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n    d.elem_begin = (size_t)d.row_begin * n_elems_root;\n    d.elem_end = (size_t)d.row_end * n_elems_root;\n    return d;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations. Each rank advances its row block on\n// its GPU; boundary rows are exchanged with neighboring ranks each iteration.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters, const Decomposition& d,\n                   const int n_elems_root) {\n    const size_t row_bytes = (size_t)n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_owned = d.elem_end - d.elem_begin;\n\n    // Each rank only stores its owned slice on the device, plus one halo row\n    // on each side of the dynamic state, so memory and transfer volume scale\n    // with 1/n_ranks.\n    const size_t alloc_begin = d.elem_begin - (d.row_begin > 0 ? (size_t)n_elems_root : 0);\n    const size_t alloc_end = d.elem_end + (d.row_end < n_elems_root && n_owned > 0\n                                               ? (size_t)n_elems_root : 0);\n    const size_t n_alloc = alloc_end - alloc_begin;\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    Material* d_materials = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dyn = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dyn_swap = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_static, std::max(n_owned, (size_t)1) * sizeof(ElementStatic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dyn, std::max(n_alloc, (size_t)1) * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dyn_swap, std::max(n_alloc, (size_t)1) * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n\n    if (n_owned > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_static, world.elements_static.data() + d.elem_begin,\n                              n_owned * sizeof(ElementStatic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn, world.elements_dynamic.data() + alloc_begin,\n                              n_alloc * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_materials, world.materials.data(),\n                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Pinned staging buffers for halo rows: [0] = row sent down/received from\n    // below (row_begin - 1), [1] = row sent up/received from above (row_end).\n    ElementDynamic* h_send = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* h_recv = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_send, 2 * row_bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recv, 2 * row_bytes));\n\n    // Ranks with no rows (size > n_elems_root) sit out the halo exchange;\n    // empty blocks only occur at the tail of the rank range, so a non-empty\n    // rank's index neighbors are always its rank neighbors.\n    const bool has_work = n_owned > 0;\n    const int prev_rank = (has_work && d.row_begin > 0) ? d.rank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    const int next_rank = (has_work && d.row_end < n_elems_root) ? d.rank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n\n    constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n    const int grid_size = has_work ? (int)((n_owned + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE) : 0;\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Refresh halo rows with neighbors' results from the previous\n        // iteration (initial state is globally consistent on every rank).\n        if (iter > 0 && d.size > 1) {\n            if (has_work) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send, d_dyn + d.elem_begin, row_bytes,\n                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send + n_elems_root,\n                                      d_dyn + d.elem_end - n_elems_root, row_bytes,\n                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            }\n            MPI_Request reqs[4];\n            MPI_Irecv(h_recv, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, prev_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[0]);\n            MPI_Irecv(h_recv + n_elems_root, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, next_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[1]);\n            MPI_Isend(h_send, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, prev_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[2]);\n            MPI_Isend(h_send + n_elems_root, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, next_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[3]);\n            MPI_Waitall(4, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n            if (prev_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn + d.elem_begin - n_elems_root, h_recv,\n                                      row_bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n            if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn + d.elem_end, h_recv + n_elems_root,\n                                      row_bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Update all owned elements\n        if (has_work) {\n            updateKernel<<<grid_size, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_static, d_dyn, d_dyn_swap,\n                                                    d_materials, d.elem_begin, d.elem_end);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n        }\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_dyn, d_dyn_swap);\n    }\n\n    // Gather all owned blocks onto every rank so the final state matches the\n    // serial code's elements_dynamic everywhere.\n    if (has_work) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data() + d.elem_begin,\n                              d_dyn + d.elem_begin, n_owned * sizeof(ElementDynamic),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n    if (d.size > 1) {\n        std::vector<int> counts(d.size), displs(d.size);\n        const int rows_per_rank = n_elems_root / d.size;\n        const int remainder = n_elems_root % d.size;\n        for (int r = 0; r < d.size; ++r) {\n            const int rb = r * rows_per_rank + std::min(r, remainder);\n            const int re = rb + rows_per_rank + (r < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n            counts[r] = (int)((size_t)(re - rb) * n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n            displs[r] = (int)((size_t)rb * n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n        }\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_BYTE, world.elements_dynamic.data(),\n                       counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_BYTE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_send));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_recv));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_static));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_materials));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dyn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dyn_swap));\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n\n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification.\n// XOR-combining is order-independent, so the loop parallelizes bit-exactly.\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    const size_t n = elements.size();\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                                    val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread: gather flux from connected elements and\n// integrate. The device only holds this rank's slice: elements_static covers\n// the owned range [elem_begin, elem_end), elements_in/out cover it plus one\n// halo row on each side starting at global index alloc_begin. Connectivity\n// indices are global and are rebased onto the slice.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elements_static,\n                             const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_in,\n                             ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_out,\n                             const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n                             const size_t elem_begin, const size_t elem_end,\n                             const size_t alloc_begin) {\n    const size_t i = elem_begin + blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= elem_end) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& elem_static = elements_static[i - elem_begin];\n    const ElementDynamic elem_dyn = elements_in[i - alloc_begin];\n    const Material mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements\n    for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n        const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n        const ElementDynamic neighbor_dyn = elements_in[neighbor_idx - alloc_begin];\n        total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    ElementDynamic& elem_write = elements_out[i - alloc_begin];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Row-block domain decomposition: rank owns grid rows [row_begin, row_end).\nstruct Decomposition {\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    int row_begin = 0;\n    int row_end = 0;\n    size_t elem_begin = 0;\n    size_t elem_end = 0;\n};\n\nDecomposition decompose(const int n_elems_root) {\n    Decomposition d;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.size);\n\n    const int rows_per_rank = n_elems_root / d.size;\n    const int remainder = n_elems_root % d.size;\n    d.row_begin = d.rank * rows_per_rank + std::min(d.rank, remainder);\n    d.row_end = d.row_begin + rows_per_rank + (d.rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n    d.elem_begin = (size_t)d.row_begin * n_elems_root;\n    d.elem_end = (size_t)d.row_end * n_elems_root;\n    return d;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations. Each rank advances its row block on\n// its GPU; boundary rows are exchanged with neighboring ranks each iteration.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters, const Decomposition& d,\n                   const int n_elems_root) {\n    const size_t row_bytes = (size_t)n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_owned = d.elem_end - d.elem_begin;\n\n    // Each rank only stores its owned slice on the device, plus one halo row\n    // on each side of the dynamic state, so memory and transfer volume scale\n    // with 1/n_ranks.\n    const size_t alloc_begin = d.elem_begin - (d.row_begin > 0 ? (size_t)n_elems_root : 0);\n    const size_t alloc_end = d.elem_end + (d.row_end < n_elems_root && n_owned > 0\n                                               ? (size_t)n_elems_root : 0);\n    const size_t n_alloc = alloc_end - alloc_begin;\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    Material* d_materials = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dyn = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dyn_swap = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_static, std::max(n_owned, (size_t)1) * sizeof(ElementStatic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dyn, std::max(n_alloc, (size_t)1) * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dyn_swap, std::max(n_alloc, (size_t)1) * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n\n    if (n_owned > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_static, world.elements_static.data() + d.elem_begin,\n                              n_owned * sizeof(ElementStatic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn, world.elements_dynamic.data() + alloc_begin,\n                              n_alloc * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_materials, world.materials.data(),\n                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Pinned staging buffers for halo rows: [0] = row sent down/received from\n    // below (row_begin - 1), [1] = row sent up/received from above (row_end).\n    ElementDynamic* h_send = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* h_recv = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_send, 2 * row_bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recv, 2 * row_bytes));\n\n    // Ranks with no rows (size > n_elems_root) sit out the halo exchange;\n    // empty blocks only occur at the tail of the rank range, so a non-empty\n    // rank's index neighbors are always its rank neighbors.\n    const bool has_work = n_owned > 0;\n    const int prev_rank = (has_work && d.row_begin > 0) ? (int)((n_owned + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE) : 0;\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Refresh halo rows with neighbors' results from the previous\n        // iteration (initial state is globally consistent on every rank).\n        if (iter > 0 && d.size > 1) {\n            if (has_work) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send, d_dyn + (d.elem_begin - alloc_begin),\n                                      row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send + n_elems_root,\n                                      d_dyn + (d.elem_end - n_elems_root - alloc_begin),\n                                      row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            }\n            MPI_Request reqs[4];\n            MPI_Irecv(h_recv, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, prev_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[0]);\n            MPI_Irecv(h_recv + n_elems_root, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, next_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[1]);\n            MPI_Isend(h_send, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, prev_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[2]);\n            MPI_Isend(h_send + n_elems_root, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, next_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[3]);\n            MPI_Waitall(4, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n            if (prev_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn, h_recv, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n            if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn + (d.elem_end - alloc_begin),\n                                      h_recv + n_elems_root, row_bytes,\n                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Update all owned elements\n        if (has_work) {\n            updateKernel<<<grid_size, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_static, d_dyn, d_dyn_swap,\n                                                    d_materials, d.elem_begin, d.elem_end,\n                                                    alloc_begin);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        }\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_dyn, d_dyn_swap);\n    }\n\n    // Gather all owned blocks onto every rank so the final state matches the\n    // serial code's elements_dynamic everywhere.\n    if (has_work) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data() + d.elem_begin,\n                              d_dyn + d.elem_begin, n_owned * sizeof(ElementDynamic),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n    if (d.size > 1) {\n        std::vector<int> counts(d.size), displs(d.size);\n        const int rows_per_rank = n_elems_root / d.size;\n        const int remainder = n_elems_root % d.size;\n        for (int r = 0; r < d.size; ++r) {\n            const int rb = sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n            displs[r] = (int)((size_t)rb * n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n        }\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_BYTE, world.elements_dynamic.data(),\n                       counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_BYTE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_send));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_recv));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_static));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_materials));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dyn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dyn_swap));\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n\n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification.\n// XOR-combining is order-independent, so the loop parallelizes bit-exactly.\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    const size_t n = elements.size();\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            int rank0;\n            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank0);\n            if (rank0 == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            int rank0;\n            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank0);\n            if (rank0 == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const Decomposition d = decompose(n_elems_root);\n    const bool is_root = (d.rank == 0);\n\n    // Bind each rank to a GPU by node-local rank\n    {\n        MPI_Comm local_comm;\n        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, d.rank,\n                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm);\n        int local_rank;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank);\n        MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);\n        int n_devices = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&n_devices));\n        if (n_devices == 0) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % n_devices));\n    }\n\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    if (is_root) {\n        printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\\n\", n_elems_root, n_elems_root, n_elems);\n        printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", n_iters);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"Parallelism: %d MPI ranks, %d OpenMP threads/rank, CUDA GPUs\\n\",\n               d.size, omp_get_max_threads());\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n\n        // Build the unstructured mesh\n        printf(\"Building unstructured mesh...\\n\");\n    }\n    World world;\n    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);\n\n    if (is_root) {\n        // Calculate memory usage\n        const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n        const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n        const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n        printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n               total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n               static_mem / (1024.0 * MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    runSimulation(world, n_iters, d, n_elems_root);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = %ld ms\\n\", duration_ms);\n\n        // Calculate performance metrics\n        const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);\n        const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;\n        const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n\n        // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)\n        const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * 22.0;\n\n        printf(\"Performance:\\n\");\n        printf(\"  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\\n\",    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                                    val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Update one element per thread: gather flux from connected elements and\n// integrate. The device only holds this rank's slice: elements_static covers\n// the owned range [elem_begin, elem_end), elements_in/out cover it plus one\n// halo row on each side starting at global index alloc_begin. Connectivity\n// indices are global and are rebased onto the slice.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elements_static,\n                             const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_in,\n                             ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_out,\n                             const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n                             const size_t elem_begin, const size_t elem_end,\n                             const size_t alloc_begin) {\n    const size_t i = elem_begin + blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= elem_end) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& elem_static = elements_static[i - elem_begin];\n    const ElementDynamic elem_dyn = elements_in[i - alloc_begin];\n    const Material mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements\n    for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n        const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n        const ElementDynamic neighbor_dyn = elements_in[neighbor_idx - alloc_begin];\n        total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    ElementDynamic& elem_write = elements_out[i - alloc_begin];\n    elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n    elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Row-block domain decomposition: rank owns grid rows [row_begin, row_end).\nstruct Decomposition {\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    int row_begin = 0;\n    int row_end = 0;\n    size_t elem_begin = 0;\n    size_t elem_end = 0;\n};\n\nDecomposition decompose(const int n_elems_root) {\n    Decomposition d;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &d.size);\n\n    const int rows_per_rank = n_elems_root / d.size;\n    const int remainder = n_elems_root % d.size;\n    d.row_begin = d.rank * rows_per_rank + std::min(d.rank, remainder);\n    d.row_end = d.row_begin + rows_per_rank + (d.rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n    d.elem_begin = (size_t)d.row_begin * n_elems_root;\n    d.elem_end = (size_t)d.row_end * n_elems_root;\n    return d;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations. Each rank advances its row block on\n// its GPU; boundary rows are exchanged with neighboring ranks each iteration.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters, const Decomposition& d,\n                   const int n_elems_root) {\n    const size_t row_bytes = (size_t)n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_owned = d.elem_end - d.elem_begin;\n\n    // Each rank only stores its owned slice on the device, plus one halo row\n    // on each side of the dynamic state, so memory and transfer volume scale\n    // with 1/n_ranks.\n    const size_t alloc_begin = d.elem_begin - (d.row_begin > 0 ? (size_t)n_elems_root : 0);\n    const size_t alloc_end = d.elem_end + (d.row_end < n_elems_root && n_owned > 0\n                                               ? (size_t)n_elems_root : 0);\n    const size_t n_alloc = alloc_end - alloc_begin;\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    Material* d_materials = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dyn = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dyn_swap = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_static, std::max(n_owned, (size_t)1) * sizeof(ElementStatic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dyn, std::max(n_alloc, (size_t)1) * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dyn_swap, std::max(n_alloc, (size_t)1) * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n\n    if (n_owned > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_static, world.elements_static.data() + d.elem_begin,\n                              n_owned * sizeof(ElementStatic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn, world.elements_dynamic.data() + alloc_begin,\n                              n_alloc * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_materials, world.materials.data(),\n                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Pinned staging buffers for halo rows: [0] = row sent down/received from\n    // below (row_begin - 1), [1] = row sent up/received from above (row_end).\n    ElementDynamic* h_send = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* h_recv = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_send, 2 * row_bytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_recv, 2 * row_bytes));\n\n    // Ranks with no rows (size > n_elems_root) sit out the halo exchange;\n    // empty blocks only occur at the tail of the rank range, so a non-empty\n    // rank's index neighbors are always its rank neighbors.\n    const bool has_work = n_owned > 0;\n    const int prev_rank = (has_work && d.row_begin > 0) ? d.rank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    const int next_rank = (has_work && d.row_end < n_elems_root) ? d.rank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n\n    constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n    const int grid_size = has_work ? (int)((n_owned + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE) : 0;\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Refresh halo rows with neighbors' results from the previous\n        // iteration (initial state is globally consistent on every rank).\n        if (iter > 0 && d.size > 1) {\n            if (has_work) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send, d_dyn + (d.elem_begin - alloc_begin),\n                                      row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_send + n_elems_root,\n                                      d_dyn + (d.elem_end - n_elems_root - alloc_begin),\n                                      row_bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            }\n            MPI_Request reqs[4];\n            MPI_Irecv(h_recv, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, prev_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[0]);\n            MPI_Irecv(h_recv + n_elems_root, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, next_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[1]);\n            MPI_Isend(h_send, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, prev_rank, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[2]);\n            MPI_Isend(h_send + n_elems_root, (int)row_bytes, MPI_BYTE, next_rank, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[3]);\n            MPI_Waitall(4, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n            if (prev_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn, h_recv, row_bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n            if (next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dyn + (d.elem_end - alloc_begin),\n                                      h_recv + n_elems_root, row_bytes,\n                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Update all owned elements\n        if (has_work) {\n            updateKernel<<<grid_size, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_static, d_dyn, d_dyn_swap,\n                                                    d_materials, d.elem_begin, d.elem_end,\n                                                    alloc_begin);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        }\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_dyn, d_dyn_swap);\n    }\n\n    // Gather all owned blocks onto every rank so the final state matches the\n    // serial code's elements_dynamic everywhere.\n    if (has_work) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data() + d.elem_begin,\n                              d_dyn + (d.elem_begin - alloc_begin),\n                              n_owned * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n    if (d.size > 1) {\n        std::vector<int> counts(d.size), displs(d.size);\n        const int rows_per_rank = n_elems_root / d.size;\n        const int remainder = n_elems_root % d.size;\n        for (int r = 0; r < d.size; ++r) {\n            const int rb = r * rows_per_rank + std::min(r, remainder);\n            const int re = rb + rows_per_rank + (r < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n            counts[r] = (int)((size_t)(re - rb) * n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n            displs[r] = (int)((size_t)rb * n_elems_root * sizeof(ElementDynamic));\n        }\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_BYTE, world.elements_dynamic.data(),\n                       counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_BYTE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_send));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(h_recv));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_static));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_materials));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dyn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dyn_swap));\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n\n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification.\n// XOR-combining is order-independent, so the loop parallelizes bit-exactly.\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    const size_t n = elements.size();\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(^:hash)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            int rank0;\n            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank0);\n            if (rank0 == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            int rank0;\n            MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank0);\n            if (rank0 == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const Decomposition d = decompose(n_elems_root);\n    const bool is_root = (d.rank == 0);\n\n    // Bind each rank to a GPU by node-local rank\n    {\n        MPI_Comm local_comm;\n        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, d.rank,\n                            MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm);\n        int local_rank;\n        MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank);\n        MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);\n        int n_devices = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&n_devices));\n        if (n_devices == 0) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % n_devices));\n    }\n\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    if (is_root) {\n        printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\\n\", n_elems_root, n_elems_root, n_elems);\n        printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", n_iters);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"Parallelism: %d MPI ranks, %d OpenMP threads/rank, CUDA GPUs\\n\",\n               d.size, omp_get_max_threads());\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n\n        // Build the unstructured mesh\n        printf(\"Building unstructured mesh...\\n\");\n    }\n    World world;\n    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);\n\n    if (is_root) {\n        // Calculate memory usage\n        const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n        const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n        const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n        printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n               total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n               static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n               dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n        printf(\"\\n\");\n\n        // Run simulation\n        printf(\"Running simulation...\\n\");\n    }\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    runSimulation(world, n_iters, d, n_elems_root);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration_ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();\n\n    if (is_root) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;\n        const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n\n        // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS The unstructured benchmark is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program, verified bit-identical to the original serial code at every configuration tested.\n\n## Design\n\nThe simulation is a Jacobi-style neighbor-gather over the mesh, so each parallel layer maps naturally:\n\n- **MPI** — row-block domain decomposition (`unstructured/unstructured.cpp:180`). Each rank owns a contiguous block of grid rows and exchanges one boundary row per side per iteration via non-blocking `MPI_Isend`/`MPI_Irecv` through pinned staging buffers. Ranks bind to GPUs by node-local rank (`MPI_Comm_split_type` + `cudaSetDevice`), so it runs correctly across multi-GPU nodes of a cluster. Ranks that get zero rows (more ranks than rows) sit out the exchange cleanly.\n- **CUDA** — the per-element flux gather and update runs as a GPU kernel, one thread per element, with connection order preserved so floating-point results are bit-exact vs. the original. Each device holds only its rank's slice plus halo rows, so memory and host-to-device traffic scale as 1/N ranks.\n- **OpenMP** — parallelizes host-side mesh construction/initialization and the result hash (the XOR-combine hash is order-independent, so the parallel reduction is bit-exact).\n\n`CMakeLists.txt` now enables the CUDA language (compiling the existing `unstructured.cpp` via `set_source_files_properties(... LANGUAGE CUDA)` — no new files, executable still `unstructured`), links `MPI::MPI_CXX` and `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`, targets the native GPU architecture, and falls back to g++-12 as the nvcc host compiler since this machine's default g++-13 is too new for CUDA 12.0.\n\n## Correctness\n\nI reconstructed the original serial code in /tmp as a reference. Result hashes (bit-exact, covering both `current_energy` and `total_flux`) match at 1, 2, 4, and 6 ranks for grids of 4², 33², 300², 512², and 1024² at various iteration counts, including the oversubscribed case with more ranks than grid rows; `-v` validation passes and `-r` output hashes are identical across rank counts.\n\n## Performance (4× RTX 3090 node)\n\n- Serial original, 8192², 50 iters: **45.7 s** → hybrid 1 rank: 1.84 s, 4 ranks: **0.74 s** (~62× total)\n- At 500 iterations on 4 GPUs the setup transfer amortizes: **11.8 GigaElements/s** (~260 GFLOPS)\n\nOne incidental fix: the elements/sec metric computed `n_measured_iters * n_elems` in `int`, which overflowed to negative values for large grids; it now accumulates in `double`."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":571,"cache_read_input_tokens":54563,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":571},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0adf9630-e7a4-427e-856b-e0f47eb1181f","uuid":"edf68875-ab6b-4cd7-ba9f-8bb67ea9fcff","timestamp":"2026-09-06T08:07:07.711Z","request_id":"req_011CemqnaR5qafdvphmBuLqw"}
